KA ' TIEN LAMPHRANC
Ha kane ka Kitab 'U Khasi bad ka Mariang Part - II lem
bad ki Ktien kynnoh' nga la pyrshang ban lum, katba nga
lah, ia ka jingdon ki lum, ki wah, ki kshaid bad kiwei kiwei
ki jingthaw bakordor ba U Blei u la buh ha ka mariang bad
ban kdew de ia ka rukom jingpyndonkam u briew ha ka
rukom kaba pher bapher. Ha kane ka Kitab, nga pyrshang
ruh ban kdewia ka rukom tih 'dewiong, mawshun, uranium,
ka rukom tih ia ki maw, u shyiap bad kiwei kiwei ki mar
poh-khyndew ha ka rukom kaba thurmur bad pathar kat
haduh ba ka pynsiew lut ia ki lum, ki wah, ki kshaid bad
pyniap ia ki jingthaw ba im ba par baroh hapoh bad ha sla
khyndew. Nalor kata, nga pyrshang ban lum bad kdew ia ka
jingdon ki jait dohkha, mrad, sim, khniang, ki jait soh,
syntiew, jhur bad kiwei kiwei ki jingpynlong U Blei kiba la
buh ha ri Khasi - Jaihtia bad kiba bun na ki ki la nangjah bad
duh tyllong, kiba ngim lah shuh ban iohi ha kane ka juk
mynta, nalor kata, ka rep ruh i kumba kan duh tyllong noh
na ka daw ka jingpynjulor u briew ia ka mariang. Nga
pyrshang ban kdew ia ka jingshisha jong kine kiei kiei da
kata ka jingthmu ban kyrsiew thiah ia ka ri bad ka jaitbynriew

ba ka hap ban pyrkhat thymmai kumno ban tei ia ka ri bad
ka jaitbynriew ha kata ka rukom ba kan neh kan skhem bad
ba ka pateng bynriew ka ban wan kan nym shem ka jingeh
bad jingjynjar bad ban sah ka nam ka burom ka
ri Khasi Jaihtia, ka ri tipbriew tipblei ba la jer la tang da u
Longshwa Manshwa.
ii
Ha kane ka Kitab nga bynrap lang de fa ki ktien kynnob
lem bad ka jingpyrshang ban batai ia ka jingmut jong ki da
kata ka jingpyrkhat ba kan long ka jingmyntoi ia ki paidbah
nongpule kot khamtam ia ki khynnah skul. Ha ka jingpyrshang
jong nga ban batai ia ki ktien kynnoh ki lah ban don ki
jingduna te hynrei nga kyrpad ia ki nongpule khamtam ki
'riewpyrkhat ba kin ai jingmut lem khnang ban nang kham
pynbha shuh shuh ia ka rukom sngewthuh jong ngi ia ki
ktien kynnoh ban nang pynroi ia ka thoh ka pule Khasi.

Rev. Dr. I. Kharkongngor
Ka Ri Khasi Jaintia ka long kawei na ki Hima ri lum kaba
khraw bad kaba i'ar shibun ha ka thain shatei lam mihngi jong ka
ri India. Kat kum ka map jong ka Khasi Jaintia Hills (Pre-British
Rule 1829), ka jingiar u pud u sam ka long naduh Silhet haduh ka
wah Bramaputra bad shaduh khap Nowgong bad ka Guwahati
lane “lew Die Kwai” kaba long ka jaka khai'i hapdeng ki Syiem
Khasi bad Syiem Ahom hyndai.
Ka Ri Khasi Jaintia ka long, da shisha, ka ri ka jingkyrkhu ha
kaba U Blei u la buh ia ki jingdonkam baroh, hapoh bad ha sla
khyndew. Ka long ka ri kaba seisoh bad kaba shlei bad syrtap da
ki mar poh khyndew kaba U Blei u la buh hapoh bad sla khyndew.
Ka long ka ri kaba dap da ka dud bad ka ngap.
Ka ri Khasi Jaintia ka long ka Hima ri lum kaba itynnat bad
kaba pyngngad, kaba dap da ki lum, ki wah, ki kshaid, ki krem,

ki khlaw ki btap, ki mrad khlaw, ki sim bad ki jingthaw ba im
laiphew jait. Ka pyniap biej ia ki phareng kiba wan sah pyngngad
ha Ri Khasi da kaba pyniasyriem ia ka ri Khasi bad ka ri Scotland
bad sa kyrteng ia ka, “Scotland of the East”.
U Blei u la buh ia ki lum, ki wah, ki kshaid ha ka rukom
kaba ryntih bad itynnad bha. Ki long kiba ithiang khamtam haba
peit na ki jaka kiba jngai. Ka khyndew kaba seisoh bad skhem, ki
maw ki i’alang lynnong lynnong halor lum, ha madan bad ha them
bad u shyiap uba bun syrtap bad kordor. Ki don ki them hapdeng
ki lum bad u palam ne lyoh khyndew u kiew arsut na them shalor
lum khamtam ha ka por slap. U slap uba biang na ka bynta ki
jynthung jyntep bad ka jingdonkam u briew. Ym ju don ka shlei
ne dap um kat ban pynjulor ia ka rep ka riang ne ia ki jingim jong
ki mrad laiphew jait. Haba baroh ki saw aiom ka ai ka jingshit
bad jingkhriat kat ban binag ha ka jingim u briew bad ki jingthaw
ba im ba par baroh. Baroh kiei kiei kiba U Blei u la buh ha Ri
Khasi Jaifitia ki long kiba dap ba biang bha te hynrei ka sah tang
ha u briew ban hiewkor bad pyndonkam hok ia ka.
2
Kat kum ka map jong ka Khasi Jaintia (Pre-British Rule 1829),
ia ka Ri Khasi Jaifltia la bynta ha ki lai tylli ki Jylli ne bynta ba
kyrpang bad kita ki long :
(1) Ka thain Ri Lum - ka don hapdeng jong ka ri bad ka long
kawei ka lynter lum ba i'asoh lang ter ter naduh ki lum Cashar
na shatei shaduh ki lum Garo.
(2) Ka thain Ri Bhoi - ka dei ka jylli ba lam noh padun shaduh
ri thor khap Kamrup, Nowgong bad karbi Anglong.
(3) Ka thain Ri War - ka dei ka jylli Ri lum ba i'eng rynsung

ter ter haba khmih na East Pakistan (ne Bangladesh kaba
mynta) bad ka long ka jaka ka dap da ki riat ki ram bad ki
patwar lane ki lyhuh kiba jylliew.
Kine lai tylli ki jylli ne ki bynta bakyrpang kiba pynbynta u
pharcng naduh Sylhet haduh Nowgong bad Guwahati kim
don jingiapher eiei ha ka jinglong jingim kum ka jaitbynriew,
ki lum ki wah, ka khyndew ka shyiap bad ka jingshit
jingkhriat. Ha ka por lyiur, u slap u jur bha bad ha ka por
tlang ka jur ka jingdait thah bad khriat.
Katto katnc shaphang (a) ki lum, (b) ki wah, (k) ki kshaid,
(d) ki khlaw.
(a) KI LUM KIBA KHAM KONGSAN :
1. Lum Shyllong : U don tang khyndiat meter shaphang
shathic na ka nongbah Shillong. U jrong 4445 leet lane 1965
meter na sla duriaw. U long u lum uba jrong tarn ha Khasi Jaifltia
Hills lane ka Jylla Meghalaya hi baroh kawei. U long u lum uba
sah jingkynmaw ha ka histori jong ka Hima Shyllong namar hajan
une u lum don ka krem Marai kaba hajan ka shnong Nongkrem na
kaba la i'oh kem ia kawei ka thei samla kaba bha briew. la kane ka
thei samla kaba bha briew la i'oh kem da u Bambteng Mylliemngap
da kaba u khroh ne pah ia ka da u syntiew pathaw khasi. Namar
kata, la khot kyrteng ia kane ka thei samla da ka Pah syntiew. La
thung syiem ia kane ka thei samla bad la seng ia ka Hima Shyllong.
3
U lum hi u long uba kyntang namar dei u lum uba shong u
‘lei-shyllong’. Ha ki por mynshwa la ju pynlong ruh ka jyngkfiia
ha une u lum. Ha sharing u Lum shyllong shaphang hynfiiew
mer, don kawei ka shnong kaba la ai kyrleng Mawkhan, kaba
haduh mynta la ong ba ka dei jaka kaba la pynlong ia ka knia ka
khriain.
U long u lum uba itvnnat, uba don nam bad uba pyngagad.
Ha ki por mynshwa, ki bricw na baroli ki thain ne na kjat u Lum

Shyllong ki ju iakiew iahiar man ka por ha maloi khlieh u Lum
Shyllong ban ia bain Iyer pyngngad bad ban ialeh kmen leh
sngewbha. Ha baroh shi lynter u Lum Shyllong ka don ka khlaw
kaba rben. Ki jait mrad, ki sim bapher bapher bad ki jait soh bad
syntiew ki don ha U Lum Shyllong.
Dang khyndiat snem lip kumno ki shipai Air Force ki ioh
ban buhai shnong ha Lum Shyllong. Kiba bun ki bynta ha u Lum
Shyllong ki long inynta ki jaka shong shipai bad tang khyndiat eh
ki sah ki jaka kiba long ban rep. l ! Lum Shyllong uba ju long
pateng la pateng u lum ba kyntang, ka jaka shong ki blei Shyllong
bad ka jaka rep, mynta u la long ka jaka shong bad ka ktem shipai.
Mynta u lum u la synlcn bad syrtap da ki ting shipai, lait noh tang
katto katne ki bynta kiba don ka khlaw sorkar bad ki briew ruh
kim ioh laitluid shuh ban i'aiaid kai pyngngad halor une u lum bad
ban pyniaid kali na san mer sha laitkor lyngba u lum-Shyllong, ka
surok ba la shna da ki phareng mynshwa.
Nalor kata, ia ka Farm kaba ju pynlong ka jaka rep bad kaba
pyndonkam ban pynmih syntbai bad ri jingri la ai noh sha ki
shipai Air Force kumba lai na ka saw bynta ban shna office bad
jaka shong (quarter) ki shipai. Kane ka Farm mynta ha Upper
Shillong kam i long shuh ban rep bad ri jingri hynrei ka shu sah
tang katto katne ki office Sorkar ban pynlong training shaphang
ka kam rep (agriculture) bad kiwei kiwei ki jingdonkam.
Ha Nongkrem ruh, ia ka jaka ba seisoh naduh lum Lwai haduh
Maw-i-sah na sha sepngi bad haduh mar syndah ka jaka Air Force
4
na shaphang shatei kaba long da ki hajar acres la pynlong ka len
shipai Assam Rifles. Ha Nongkrem ruh kumba mar shiteng ki
jaka kiba long ban rep la ioh pyndonkam ki shipai Assam Rifles.

Lada lah ki kwah hi ban i'oh syndon ia ka Nongkrem baroh kawei
ban pynlong ka len shipai kaba heh.
Ha Shillong ruh i’a ki jaka ne khyndew la die sha ki shipai na
ka bynta ban pynlong len shipai ne ka Head Quarter jong ki shipai.
La die ia ka khyndew tang Rs. 2000 (Ar hajar tyngka) sha ka
syiem Bilat da u Melay Sing (Syiem Mylliem) bad u Raja Rabon
(Syiem khyrim) bad la sakhi da ki saw ngut ki myntri Mylliem
bad saw ngut ki myntri Khyrim. Ia kane ka khyndew Ia die sha ka
Syiem Bilat lyngba u lieutenant Colonel Haughton, Governor
General’s Agent North East Frontier bad la iasoi i'a ka Agreement
ha ka 8 ,h December, 1863.
Ka khyndew kaba la die ka long kumne : “ the land
at Shillong known as the Shillong lands; the Raj lands South of
the Oom Soopree, known as the Kurkontang Nongkseh land; the
land near youdoe; known as Shillong Labang land’s " By
J. N. Chowdhury.
2. U Lum Rapleng : U don sha mihngi na Nongkrem. U
him uba lah ban i'ohi khyndiang na ki thain ri War bad katto katne
na ki thaifi Jaintia. U long u lum uba synlen bad pyngngad. Ki
briew na ki thain Nongkrem, Smit, Thangsning bad kiba ia marjan
ki ju ioh ban ia leit kiew pyngngad halor une u lum khamtam ha
ka por synrai. Halor une u lum ruh mynta ym don shuh ban ialeit
bam Iyer pyngngad namar la dap da ki kor ki bor ba pyndonkam
u briew kat kum ka juk mynta.
3. U Lum Diengiei : U don sha shiliang ka wah Umiam
shaphang sepngi na ka nongbah Shillong. La khot Lum Diengiei
namar ka don ka dieng bad kaba khot “Diengiei”. La ong ba ka
long ka dieng kaba mih nyngkong eh ha lum diengiei bad la ong
ruh ba ka jingheh jong ka la biang ban kah symgiew lut ia ka
pyrthei hi baroh kawei. Haduh mynta ha maloi khlieh u lum
5

Diengiei don ka thliew kaba heh bad iar ha kaba la ong ba ka ^ ei
ka tyllong ba i'eng ka diengiei.
Ha u lum Diengiei ka don ka khlaw kaba heh, kaba iar bad
rben. Katba nang mih ki sngi bad nang Va id ki por, u khun bynriew
u pom pathar i'a ka khlaw bad u lum u kylla synlen bad kaba sah
mynta sa tang ki diengkseh phyrngop hangne hangtai bad ka ’dew
ra. Ka khyndew kaba ju long mynno mynno kaba sbur bad seisoh,
mynta ka la kylla long ’dew ksang; ki umpohliew kiba tuid arsut
na lum Diengiei sha wah Umiam ki la rngat lut; kan poi ka por ba
kan don ka jingkyrduh um kaba khraw ha lum Diengiei bad ki
briew ruh mian pa mian ki nang sakma had phet rieh sha ki thaifl
ri Bhoi ban rep bad kyndoi ja kpoh i'a lade.
U lum Diengiei, lada shim ia ka lyntcr bad ka pyngkiang
naba kut shaba kut, u long kawei ka ’dew lynnong rit. Ka don ka
lad ban thung dieng biang bad pynsan sa shisien i'a ka khlaw ha
lum Diengiei bad na une u lum iah ban bsa um biang i'a ka dam ha
Umiam khwan.
4. Lum Sohpet bneng : U don shaphang shatei na ka
nongbah Shillong sha shiliang kamon ha lynti ban leit na Shillong
sha Guwahati. U jrong kumba 28 kilo meter. Ka khana-pateng ka
iathuh ba u lum Sohpet bneng u long ka jaka i'akynduh hapdeng
ka bneng bad ka khyndew lyngba kata ka jyngkieng ksiar hyndai
ha kaba i'a kiew bad i'a hiar hapdeng ki Khyndai Trep khyndai
skum kiba shong ha bneng bad ki Hynfiiew Trep hynfiiew skum
kiba shong ha khyndew.
La ong ba u lum sohpet bneng u long u lum ba ki mrad
laiphew jait ki juh ban shong, kum ka dngiem, u khla, ki sniang
khlaw, ki miaw khlaw bad kiwei. Ynda haba u lum u la dkhaw ne
synlen, ki mrad khlaw bad ki sim laiphew jait ki phet noh bad

wad ia ka jaka rieh ha khlaw khri syni'a bad don ruh kiba phet sha
ri dkhar.

Ki long ki luin kiba paw bha ha ka geography jong ka ri
Khasi Jaintia bad ki long ruli ki lum kiba don la ka jong ka jong
ka histori bad ka jingitynnat. Kine ki lum bad kiwei pat ki lum
kiba kham rit bad kham lyngkot kiba pynwan dur i'a ka jingitynnat
jong ka ri Khasi Jaintia. Ha kine ki lum don ki khlaw kiba rben.
Ki mrad, ki jait sim, u khniang bad ki jingthaw laiphew jait ki
shong ha kine ki lum. Ila kine ki sngi kiba kham bun na kine ki
lum ki la dkhaw ne synlen. Lada kine ki lum ki kylla long noh
tang ka jaka rep, jaka shong briew bad ki jaka ban kynton i'a ki
kor ki bor machine bapher bapher na ka bynta ka jingdonkam u
briew. Khlem pep ki ri baroh kawei kan duh noh ka nam bad ka
jingpaw nam jong ka kum kawei na ki ri lum kiba itynnat tam ha
sla khyndew.

(b) KATTO KATNE KI WAH KIBA KHAM KONGSAN :
1. Ka wah Umiam Khwan : Ka mih na ki thaifi Sohiong
bad Nongthliew, ka tuid lyngba i'a ki them Wei-lyngkut, Nongbsap,
Lawmei, Nongmadan bad ka noh arsut haduh Umi'am khwan. Ha
shwa ban den ia ka dam, ka long ka wah kaba sngur bad shngiam
ban dih. Ki ’kha saw bad kiwei kiwei ki jait dohkha kiba ioh na
kane ka wah ki long kiba khleih, ba shngiam bad ba bang ban
bam. Ka shnong Umsaw ka don harud ka wah Umi'am ha ka surok
rim ban leit sha Gauhati. Hadien ba la den ia kane ka wah ban
pynlong Dam, kane ka shnong ka shu ngop noh hapoh ka um.
Kiba bun ki pynthor kba roll kiba siesoh kiba don ha kane ka thaih

Umiam khwan ki ngop lut hapoh um. Haba den i'a ka um ban
pynlong Dam ka don ka jingmyntoi ban pynioh bor ding i'a la ka
jong ka ri bad shabar roh hynrei ka long pat ka jingduh kaba
khraw ha ka liang ka rep ka riang nalor ba ka ri Khasi Jaintia ka
don tang khyndiat eh ka jaka rep.
Ka Umi'am kaba ju long mynno mynno ka um kaba sngur ka
la kylla long noh ka um kaba tngit bad jaboh. Ka daw jong kane
ka long namar ki um jakhlia, u niut, ka plastic bad kiwei kiwei na
nongbah Shillong ki tuid bad pynlang ha Dainsite. Lada ym lah
ban khang ia ka jingpyntuid ia kine ki jakhlia na Sor Shillong bad
lada ym pynkhuid, ha ki phew snem ki ban wan kane ka Damsite
ka lah ban kylla long ka jaka theh niut ka Sor Shillong bad ka bor
ding ruh ka lah ban duh noh. Ki briew ki la nang jyllei, bad ka
nongbah ka la nangheh bad iar bad ka jingjaboh roh kan nang jur .
2. Ka wah Umiam Mawphlang : Ka mih na Langkyndur
na trai u Lum Shillong ka tuid arsut sha ki thaifi Nongkrem, Smit,
Nongkynrih, Mawpynthih bad poi ha Mawphlang. Kane ka wah
ka don nam na ka jingkhuid, jingsngur bad bun ki ’khasaw bad
kiwei kiwei ki jait dohkha.
Kane ka wah kaba ju long mynno mynno ka wah kaba heh
bad jylliew ka la kylla long mynta tang i wahduid iba tngit bad
jaboh. Ka jingpom pathar i'a ki khlaw baroh shi lynter ka wah
9
Umiaw haduh ban da kylla lum synlcn bad ka jingtih bad khlong
jhulum i'a u shyiap um na poll wah ka la pvnkylla long ia ka wah
kumba kan rngat noh khamtani ha ka por tlang. Ha kine ki sngi ka
don ka jingkhlong shyiap kaba jur napoh wah bad na lum ruli
harud wah, ter ter naduh smit shaduh wail l Jimyngar ha Mylliem,

la pynlong i'a ka wah tang ka um ktich. 1 la ka por tlang lei lei lada
wieh ne buh i'a ka kjat ha ka wall kan sop tang da ka ktieh. Ki jait
dohkha bad ki khhiang bapher bapher kiba sliong liapoh wah i
kumba kin duh jait lut. Ka wall kaba ju long myuuo mynno kaba

4. Ka Myntdu-Leshka Project : Ka Myntdu-Leshka Project
ka long kawei na ki project kaba donkam ban den ia ka wah na ka

pher bapher - kiba la don ka jingnang jingstad ne kyrdan pule ki

kam shoh maw, tih shyiap bad bun kiwei pat ki jait kam kuli

ka project. Haba wan ka project ki paidbah ki ioh ka surok bad
bun kiwei pat ki jait jingtei kiba iadei bad ka project.
Ha kawei pat ka liang, ka jingtei ia ka project ka ktah bun ia
ki jaka kiba long ban rep bad bym long rep. Ka donkam da ki
spah acres ban pynlong kam ia ka project. Haba la pyntrei kam ia
ka project, katto katne ki briew kiba la nang la stad ki lah ban ioh
kam office hynrei kiba bun ki nongrep kin kyrduh kam namar
bym don shuh ka jaka ban rep. Kaba mih na kata, kiba bun ki

nongrep ki hap ban kynriah noh sha kawei pat ka shnong na ka

long tang kumta arphew na ka shispah bynta. Mynta haba la nang
pynbun ki project bad nang pynjur ka jingtih ia ki mar poh khyndew
ha kylleng ka ri, ka khyndew ne jaka kaba long rep ka lah ban sah
sa tang san ne shiphew na ka shispah bynta. Hato ka jaitbynriew
kam donkam shuh ia ka khyndew ban rep jingrep?

KA RI KHASI JAINTIA :
Ki dohkha kiba ngi ioh ban tong ne khwai na ki wah ka ri
Khasi Jaintia ki long kiba khlein, kiba bang bad shngiam. Ki don
ki dohkha kiba rit bad don ruh kiba heh. Haduh kine ki sngi ki
dang don ki dohkha kiba heh haduh san bad shiphew kilo khamtam
ha ka wah Umngot bad Wahkhen. Ki jait dohkha kiba ngi lah ban
ioh na ki wah ka ri Khasi Jaintia ki long: ’kha-babia, ’klia-baifi,
’kha-bawa, ’kha-blang, ’kha-bneng, ’kha-niang, 'kha-bsein, ’kha-
khla, ’kha-kok, ’kha-koi, ’kha-kot, ’kha-kur, ’kha-kusia, ’kha-
hur, ’kha-lad, ’kha-l^so, ’kha-mukur, ’kha-saw, ’kha-ski, ’kha-
shalynnai, ’kha^shitor, ’kha-shop, ’kha-snad, ’kha-stieh, ’kha-
tara, ’kha-thli, ’kha-tlong, ’kha-tyngkra bad kiwei kiwei ki jait
dohkha rit kiba ym lah ban jer kyrteng lut hangne. (khmih na ka
‘Dienshonhi’). Kitei ki jait dohkha bad kiwei kiwei ki jait khniang
kiba im ha ki wah ki la jan duh jait lut namar kiba bun ki wah ki
la sdang rngat bad kiwei pat ki wah ki la shah tap ha u shyiap,
’dewiong, mawshun bad kiwei pat ki mar poh khyndew. Shuh
shuh, ki khlaw kiba rben kiba ju bsa uin la ki wah ki la synlen lut.
Nalor kata, ki dohkha kiba dang don katto katne ha ki wah, ki
briew ki pyniap dusmon da kaba shoh da u kynbat kharew, ka
shun, u tutia bad kiwei kiwei ki jait kynbat.
Kumba long mynta, ki dohkha kiba lah ban ioh bad
pyndonkam na ri Khasi Jaifitia ki long sa tang kito kiba ri ha ki
pung dohkha,' kiba pyndonkam ban bam mania ka sngi ki long
kiba wallam na ri dkhar.

Kat kum ka report ha ka kot khubor “U Mawphor” dated
7 th Lber 2007, ka Meghalaya State Pollution Central Board ka
pynshai paidbah halor ka jingiap ki dohkha ha wah Lukha da kaba

ka um ’dewiong sha katei ka wah na ki jaka lum thup ia u ’dewiong
naduh na Myndihati ter ter haduh ka wah Lunai kaba don mawshun.

KA SPAH KABA DON HA KI KHLAW KA RI KHASI
jaiStia
Ka ri Khasi Jaifida ka long ka ri kaba riewspah ha ka jingdon
Id mar khlaw. Ki don ki khlaw Idba ngi Idiot ki ‘khlaw diengkseh’,
Id ‘khlaw diengmet’, bad ki ‘khlaw kiba dap da ki siej bad ki
shken. Ha Id khlaw ki don Id jait dieng Idba heh bad jrong bad
don ruh kiba rit bad lyngkot. Ki don Id dieng Idba rem dor bad
don ruh kiba tad dor. Ki don Id dieng kiba eh bad don ruh Idba

jem met. Nalor Id dieng, don ki jait mrad kiba pher bapher, Id
jait sim kiba phylla, ki jait khfliang lai phewjait, ki thapbalieh ba
bun tong bad ba kordor, ki jait kynbat kiba long dawai bad bym
long dawai, ki soh khlaw kiba bang bad shngiam bad bun Id jait
jhur bad jait jynthung kiba shu long hi ha khlaw.
Kumba long mynta ka spah kaba khraw eh ka long ka
jingpynmih dieng sawdong, diengkseh bad diengmet kaba ju
pyndonkam ha kaba shalan shabar lcylleng Id jylla ka ri India.
Kiwei pat Id jait dieng kiba pynloh spah Is ka jaitbynriew Khasi
Id long: Id diengblei (sal tree) diengbti, diengrai, diengtusei,
diengsning, diengngan, diengngai, diengngar, dknglteng,
di en gs ohlia . diengsohmanir, diengsohphan, dieagrting; Id long

shna ring, shna shuki, sbna miej, almari had Idwri kiwei ki
jin gd onkam . Kiwei pat Id dieng kum ki dieng lamald, diengsohli-
Is, diengsoh-mylleng, dieng-jalwankfoapynmihldilabadkiaoh
kiba pyndonkam naka bynta ban airong la ldjaiflkinep. Id khfcw

bad kiwei kiwei ki tiar, ban shna dawai bad kiwei kiwei ki

ja-iing, sim snad, sim leit Yew, sim shaiil bthuh, ka tyut, ka saifi,
ka syiar khlaw, ka sim sier, u iar tung, iar khiar, shyrwat-Yong
bad bthuh, ka sim khar bad don bun kiwei kiwei pat ki sim kiba
wan na ri dkhar kiba shongkai shipor bad ki phet noh.
Ki mrad kiba lah ban shem ha ki khlaw ka ri Khasi Jaifitia ki
long: U khla, ka dngiem, u hati, ka sier, u bthong, ka skei, u
myrsiang, u niangkhlaw, u miaw khlaw, u ksar, u niang khied, u

bsong, u bshad, tngaw, niangbri, u phyllad, ka kyrbei, u ’la
thapsim, ka rabit, u ksih, kui, rising-dieng bad risng khyndew, ki
khnai (ki don bun rukom) bad don bun kiwei kiwei pat ki mrad
kiba ngim lah ban fliew kyrteng lut.
Nalor kine kiei kiei kiba ngi la fliew kyrteng haneng, ngi
shem ruh bun jait ki khfiiang, jait skaifi bad kiwei pat ki kynja
jingthaw ba im ba par kiba shong ha ki khlaw ha ka ri Khasi
Jaifitia.
Haba ki khlaw ki shong rben, ka Yarap ban bat Ya ka jingsyaid,
Ya u slap bad ka Iyer. Ka jingdon jong ki khlaw ka Yarap ruh ban
pyniaid beit Ya ki aiom kumba ki long. Ha kito ki phew snem kiba
la leit noh ngi dang Yoh mad Ya ki saw aiom, ka pyrem, ka lyiur,
ka synrai bad ka tlang. Ngi dang Yohmad Ya ki jhur khlaw kiba
bang ba shngiam, ngi dang Yohsngew Ya ka jingkynud ki khfiiang,
Ya ka jingpah sngewtynnat ki sim, ka jingpyrta ki mrad khlaw, ka
jingkhih win ki jakoid. Ngi dang Yoh mad Ya ki umpohliew kiba
mih na ki lum bad mawsiang, ngi dang Yoh jngi ha ki pung, ki
phud wah bad ki wah bah bad ngi 'iohsngew ruh hi ka jingbyrthen
ki kshaid bad kumta ter ter.
31
(h) KA REP KA RIANG BAD KA JINGKYLLA KA
RUKOM REP :
Ha ki spah bad ki phew snem kiba la leit noh, ka jingtrei
kaba kongsan tam ha Ri Khasi Jaifitia ka long ka rep. Ha kitei Id
snem kim pat don lane kim pat sngewthuh ban trei ia kiwei pat Id
jait kam. Ki don kiba trei ia kiwei pat ki jait kam hynrei kiba bun
ki im da ka rep ka riang. Ha ki kitei ki snem, ka khyndew ka long
kaba sbur bad seisoh bha bad ki briew Id lah ban im pahuh pahai
tang da ka kam rep wat la kim don kiwei pat Id jait jingtrei. Lah
ban thung bun ki jait jynthung kynthup ia u soh u pai bad Id jhur

ki jhep laiphew jait. Ki jingrep kiba kham kongsan ki long u kba,
u riewhadem, krai, u phan myngngor bad phan karo, ka shriew,
u phan dieng bad bun jait ki jait jhur. Ha ki thaifl ri War bad ri
Bhoi, ki jynthung bakham kongsan ki long u kba, u kwai, u
tympew, u soh niamtra, u soh mrit khlaw, ka shriew bad kiwei
kiwei. Ha ki thaifl Jaifltia, u kba, u soh niamtra bad bun kiwei pat
ki jait jhur. Ha ki thaifl pdeng ka ri Khasi, u phan karo, u phan
myngngor, phan dieng, kba, u riew hadem, u krai bad ki jait jhur
lai phewjait. Ha ki thaifl Bhoi bad Jaifitia, ka jur bha ka rep kba
bad ka biang ban pyndap ia ka jingdonkam ha ri Khasi Jaifitia. U
synsar uba ju long mynno mynno u mar khlaw, u kylla long mynta
uwei na ki jynthung bakongsan uba bsa ia ki nongrep khamtam ha
ki thaifl ri War bad ri Bhoi.
Ka rukom rep :
Ha ri Khasi Jaifltia ka rukom rep bad ka jingpyndonkam ia u
symbai ka long ha kajuh ka rukom ha ka ri baroh kawei lait noh
ha ki katto katne ki thaifl, kum ka thaifl Bhoi, Lyngngam bad
Jaifltia ha ki jaka ba dang rben ka khlaw ki pyndonkam ka rep
jhum bad shyrti, ha ki thaifl War ki pyndonkam ki rep bri ne
bsah, bad ha ki jaka pdeng ka ri Khasi Jaifltia ki pyndonkam bun
rukom Id jait jingrep.
1. Ka rep Jhum : Ka rep jhum ka long kawei na ki rukom rep
kaba pynsyrwa ia ka khlaw, kaba pynsynlen hi ki lum Id
32
them bad kaba pynkylla ’dew rkhiang ia ki jaka ba seisoh.
Haba rep jhum donkam ban pom pynngat ne pynkyllon
na phang ia ki dieng ki siej ha khyndew naduh kiba rit tam
haduh ki diengbah. Ka shim por slem slem ban iap bad
tyrkhong kine ki dieng. Nangta sa thang da ka ding haduh
ban da kylla dpei. Ynda la dei ka aiom bet, sa puh bad kynroi

ia ka ’dew dpei bad sa thung jynthung ne sa bet !a u symbai.
Ka rep jhum ka long tang ha ki jaka ba rben bha ka khlaw
bad ka shim por da ki phew snem ban pynlong khlaw pat.
Shisien ba la rep jhum kato ka jaka ka sah shrah bad synlen
da ki phew phew snem. Ha kane ka juk mynta ka rep jhum ka
la nangduh nangduh wat haduh ki thaifi ba kyndong tam jong
ka ri Khasi Jaifltia na ka daw bym don khlaw shuh kaba biang
ban rep jhum. Ka rep jhum ka kham seisoh bun bun shah ban
ia kiwei pat ki rukom rep tangba ka long tang shisien thung
ne shisien pynhap symbai bad nangta lada rep biang ha kajuh
ka jaka kam seisoh shuh kumba mynshwa namar ka dpei kaba
mih na kaba thang ia ka khlaw ka la kylla khyndew noh bad
ka biang tang shu rep syllai.
2. Ka rep Shyrti : Ka rep shyrti ka long kumjuh kum ka rep
jhum. Ka jingiapher ka long ba ha ka rep jhum donkam ban
pom pynkyllon naphang ia ki dieng naduh kiba rit haduh ki
diengbah katba ha ka rep shyrti ki pynsah teng ia ki dieng
bah ne ki dieng heh ha kata ka rukom ba kito ki dieng kiba
sah kin kha symbai pat ban pynlong khlaw biang. Ka rukom
rep bad ki symbai ki long kumjuh kum ka rep jhum. Ha ka
rep jhum la shu thang pathar ia ka khlaw katba bam ka ding
katba ha ka rep shyrti ka don da ka pud ban thang ia ka
khlaw. Ka shyrti khlaw ka long kawei pat ka rukom thang
hi ka khlaw ban iada na ka jingpur ding sha kawei pat ka
khlaw.
3. Ka rep Bun : Ka rep bun ka long kawei pat ka rukom rep ha
kaba thang la ka khlaw. Ka iapher shibun na ka rep jhum ne
33
rep shyrti. Ha ka rep bun, kano kano ka jaka ne lum kaba

long khlaw lah ban rep bun - ym da donkam ka khlaw kaba
rben. Haba rep bun donkam ban prat lut ia u phlang, ki dieng
ki siej bad ki niut ki flier kiba don ka khlaw bad sharud khlaw
ruh. fa ki dieng kiba rit dei ban pynkyllon lut baroh bad i'a ki
dieng kiba kham heh la ju ieh noh bad tyngkai - ym baroh.
Borabor, i'a ki dieng kiba heh la shu ngor lane mait tang ia ki
tnat bad ieh noh ia ki tnat kliar khnang ba ki dieng kin im bad
kin bet symbai pat ynda la dep rep bun.
Ynda la dep prat ne khet ia ka khlaw baroh kawei kat kum ka
jingdonkam ban rep, la ju ieh noh katto katne ta'iew ba ka
sngi kan shat bad ba ki dieng ki siej ne u phlang kin rkhiang
bad kin iap noh. Ynda ki la sdang ban kem saw bad rkhiang
ne lam tyrkhong bad iap, ka la dei ka por ban lum ia ki
lynnong lynnong ban pynlong nur (bed seed). Borabor, ia ka
nur la ju shna ha ka dur kum ka tusok thiah kaba ka jingheh
ka long kumba lai phut, ka jingrben kumba shi phut bad ka
lynter ka long borabor hapdeng phra bad shi phew phut. Halor
ka nur la tap da u stong phlang uba stang bad halor u stong
pat la ju tap da ka khyndew met kaba rkhiang bad hadien
haba u stong bad ka khyndew ki la tyrkhong bad rkhiang bha
sa thang da ka ding haduh ban da long dpei. Ia une u dpei la
khot ka ‘bun’ ne u 'bun*. Ha ki phew snem kiba la leit noh,
bunsien, halor u bun la ju thung da u phan myngngor bad
phan karo, nangta sa pynbud ban thung da u jhur lai phewjait.
Na ki rukom rep baroh, ka rep bun ka long kaba siesoh shibun
tangba ka long ka kam kaba sani bad lut ksan palat.
4. Ka rep Khylliap ne rep Khlip : Ka rep khylliap ka long

kawei na ki rukom rep bun kaba duna ka jinglut hynrei kaba
shitom haduh katta katta ha ka rukom trei. Ban rep khylliap
donkam ban ioh ka jaka kaba madan, kaba long ryntih, kaba
long dewmet bad kaba long ryntih u ’langsoh (phlangsoh),
kam pher Ia ka long ka jaka kaba heh ne kaba rit, ka long kat
34
lnim kajingdonkam . Ka jaka kaba khleh khyndew bad shyiap,
kaba long dieng, kaba khohruh khohram bad kaba don ki
thied dieng, kaba long tyrkhang bad kiwei ki kynja fluit kiba
kyllaifl bad suh thied kam long satia ban rep khylliap ne khlip.
Ki mohkhiew ki dei ban long kiba nep, kiba stang bad kiba
heh. Tang ki briew kiba tbit bad mlien i'a kane ka rukom rep
ki lah ban leh bad kam long ka rukom rep kaba lah ban i'a rep
paidbah, nalor kata, ka long ruh kaba shitom ban ioh ka jaka
kaba madan khamtam ha kane ka ri lum jong ngi.
Shwa ban rep, donkam ban pynkhuid i'a kata ka jaka kaba
mut ban rep bad dei ban till tang ia ki ’langsoh kiba soh ha ka
’dewmet. i'a kane ka rukom rep la ju sdang borabor naduh u
’Naiwieng ter ter. Ban pynlong nur (seed bed), u nongrep un
tih ne soi ia ka khyndew kaba sop phlang (ban pynlong stong)
ha ka rukom kaba stang bha bad ka jingheh kan long kat ka
pliang bam ja ne tarn, un khylliap i'a kata ka khyndew ne
stong kaba sop phlang halor ka khyndew kaba don phlang,
uwei sha kamon bad uwei sha kadiang, bad ka lynter jong
kata ka nur kan long katkum ka jinglong jong ka jaka tangba
ym palat i'a ka shiphew pruh.
Ia kata ka khyndew ne stong kaba la dep soi la ju ieh haduh ar
ne lai bnai haduh ba ka khyndew kan da rkhiang bha bad u
phlang uba sop ia ka khyndew ruh un da iap bha bad tyrkhong.
Ha kaba kut artat u bnai Kyllalyngkot ne ha ka taiew kaba

nyngkong u Rymphang ka Ia dei ka por ban thang ia kata ka
khyndew ne u stong. Ha ka por ban thang artat donkam kumba
shi khoh ne ar khoh-rynnip u ftiuh-kseh ne ’langiap ban buh
hapeng ka nur. Nangta sa tap pyni'eng ia u stong ha ka dur
kum ka khoh-rynnip bad sa ai ding. Ynda la rhem bha ka
ding sa tap da ka khyndew kaba la tih bad pynrkhiang lypa
bad sa ieh noh ba ka ding kan bam i'a ka khyndew ne stong
bad suki suki uta u stong u sa kylla bun. Haba ka khyndew ka
la kylla bun, sa thung ia kino kino ki symbai katba sngew
donkam. Kane ka rukom rep ka long kaba shitom bad bniah
bha hynrei kaba duna pat ka jingpynlut ha ka rukom trei.
5. Ka rep Syllai : Ka syllai ka thew la ka khyndew ha ka jaka
lum kaba la dep rep bun - kaba dang shu rep bun shen lane
kaba la dep rep bun la kham stem. Namar ka syllai ka long ka
khyndew kaba la dep rep bun kumta ka rukom trei ka long
kaba kham suk. Haba rep syllai donkam ban tih lane kynroi
ia ka khyndew shalor ba kan pra bad jem. Ynda la dep tih
iehnoh katto katne por ia ka khyndew ba kan 'ioh sngi bad
Iyer. Ha ka por ba thung ia u phan donkam ban ber katto
katne ka sboh masi lane kano kano ka sboh bad sa bynrap ban
ber khyndiat ka sboh dawai. Ha ka por mynshwa ba dang
sbur ka khyndew, ym ju pyndonkam ia ka sboh dawai. Ia u
phan uba thung ha ka khyndew dei ban ieh tang hapdeng
khyndai phew bad shispah san sngi bad sa khlaw noh. Lada
ieh slem palat ia ka por, u phan u lah ban pyut noh. Ka
jingtrei ha ka rep syllai ka kham suk shibun bad ka jingmih u
phan ruh ka duna shibun ban ia ka rep bun. Kumba long ka

rep bun, ka rep syllai ruh lah ban thung ban jait ki jait jhur,
kum u kubi, u muli bad bun kiwei pat ki jait jhur. Lah ruh
ban rep kba uba ju khot u ’ba-syllai tangba ka jingmih ka
duna shibun na u kba-pynthor ne u kba-bun.
6 . Ka rep Kper : Ka kper ka dei ka khyndew ne ka jaka kaba
don mar syndah ne jngai khyndiat na ka iing shong. Katto
katne snem mynshwa, ha nongkyndong kawei pa kawei ka
ling ka don ka kper kaba heh kat ban biang ban rep shi bymi
ne ar byrni u phan lem bad ka rep jhur na ka bynta ka
jingdonkam ban bam ha iing bad ban ioh die ha lew ban ioh
bai siew ha ki sngi lew. Ka rep kper ka long ka jingtrei kaba
rit kat ban biang ban trei mynstep bad janmied bad haba lait
por ban trei. Katba ka rep syllai, rep bun bad rep pynthor ka
long ka jingtrei kaba heh ka ban biang ban pynim ia ka ung
ka sem. Ka rukom rep kper ka iasyriem kumjuh bad ka rep
syllai, ka jingiapher ka long ba ha kper lah ban rep baroh saw
aiom katba ba ka rep syllai lah ban rep tang ha ka aiom lyiur
bad synrai. Ka daw jong kane ka long ba ha kper lah ban ker
bad i'ada na ka jingwan tur ki masi, blang bad sniang, katba
ha ka rep syllai ym lah satia ban leh kumta namar ka jngai na
ka ting ne shnong. Mynshwa, nalor ka rep, ki nongrep, ki ri
ruh i'a ki masi, ki blang bad kiwei kiwei ki jingri. Ia kine ki
jingri khamtam i'a ka masi bad blang ki ju sharai bad pahara
bha ha ka aiom rep katba ha ka por tlang ki shu pyllait lan
khlem nong sharai.
7. Ka rep Pynthor : Ha ri Khasi Jaintia tang khyndiat eh ki
nongrep kiba pyndonkam ka rep pynthor, kata ruh kiba kham
bun ki dei tang ha ri Bhoi bad Jaintia. Ka daw jong kane ka

long ba ha ri lum Khasi Jaintia tang khyndiat ki jaka kiba
long ktieh bad lang um kiba bit ban pynlong pynthor. U kba
pynthor u donkam shibun ka um bad ka sboh. Ha ka jaka ba
tyrkhong um, u kba um da long bha. Ka pynthor ka long ka
jaka kaba pyndonkam tang ka rep kba bad u kba ruh u seisoh
bha. Don katto katne ki nongrep kiba pyndonkam ka rep
phan bad jhur ha pynthor khamtam ha ka por dang.
8. Ka rep Bri ne rep Bsah : Ka rep bri lane rep bsah ka long ka
rukom rep ha ki thaifl Riwar, War Jaintia bad katto katne ha
ki thaifi ri Bhoi. Ha baroh shi lynter ki thaifi border bad ka
Bangladesh ka jur bha ka rep kwai, tympew, sohniamtra, la
tyrpad, soh mrit khlaw, synsar bad kiwei pat ki jait jingrep.
Kane ka rukom rep kam da ktah shibun ki jinglut kum ka rep
kba bad repjhum hynrei ka long ka jingrep kaba ai jingmyntoi
shikatdei ia ka ri bad ka jait bynriew. Ha ka por ba rem kwai,
lada die ha Yew, ka dor u kwai ar byrni ka ia ryngkat bad shi
kali (trok) ka dor u phan. Kiwei kiwei ki jait jingrep haba
kim long lane kim seisoh, ki da khate ia ka bai trai bad duhnong
bad bun kiba hap ban sangeh noh ka jingrep. U kwai, ha
kawei pat ka liang, u lah ban don ka snem kaba kham soh bha
37
bad ka snem kaba duna soh te hynrei um ktah ei ei ka
jingduhnong ia ki nongrep. U long u jait jingrep uba shisien
ba la thung la shu kheit beit mania ka snem, lada shibun ne
khyndiat. Ka jingduh nong ka long tang haba iaptram bad
kata ruh ym ju kham jia kynrei. U kwai, ka latyrpad, u
sohfiiamtra, u sohphan bad kiwei kiwei ki jait soh shisien ba
la thung la shu kheit beit hynrei donkam ban ap slem haduh
ba kin da san bad seisoh. Ka shim hapdeng khatsan haduh

arphew snem haduh ban da ioh kheit soh. Ka jingmih na u
kwai ha ka shi snem shi snem ha ka ri Khasi Jaifitia ka long
da ki klur. Ka Agriculture Department ka la dei ban pynshhur
i'a ki nongrep ban rep bun u kwai ban pynriewspah i'a ka ri
bad ka jaitbynriew. U kwai u long u jait jingrep uba lah ban
pyniaid Yew kylleng ka ri India bad shabar ri.
Ha ki spah snem bad ki phew snem kiba la leit noh, ha ri
Khasi Jaifttia, ki briew ki im tang da ka rep, kumba la kdew sha
khmat, bad ia ka khyndew ruh la ju pyndonkam tang na ka bynta
ban thaw i'ing thaw sem bad bad rep ban riang. Ka khyndew ka
sbur bha bad ki briew ki ioh ban pyndonkam laitluid ia ka khyndew
ban rep.
i'a ka ri Khasi Jaifitia la tip kum ka ri lum. Kumba long lem
kiwei pat ki ri lum, ka long ka ri kaba dap da ki riat ki ram, ki
krem ki kroh, ki mawsiang mawbah bad ki maw khohrum khohram
laiphew jait bad u shyiap. Nalor kata, ha ri Khasi Jaifitia kiba bun
ki jaka kiba dap da ki mar poh khyndew, kum u dewiong, u
mawshun, silimanite, dew byrta, copper, kaolin, lead bad Zinc.
Ha kine ki sngi la shem ruh sa u Uranium bad bun kiwei pat ki
mar poh khyndew. Namar kata, haba phai pat sha ki jaka kiba
bit ban rep ban riang ki duna haduh katta katta, ka lah ban
long tang kumba arphew bynta na ka shispah bynta ka
khyndew ha ka ri baroh kawei kaba long ban rep. Mynta lei
lei haba la jur ka tih dew’iong, tih mawshun, tih shyiap bad
silimanite bad haba la nang pynjur ban tei ki Dam pynmih bor
38
ding elektrik, ki karkhana bad kiwei kiwei ki jingshna kat kum ka
juk mynta la nang duna shuh shuh ka jaka rep. Ki jaka rep ba
seisoh, kum ka Um'iam Hydel Project, Umtrew, Kyrdem kulai,

Kupli ka la ktah da ki hajar acre la ki jaka rep bad da ki hajar ki
noitgrep ki la duh jaka ban rep bad la ki lingshong jong ki rub la
hap ban pynkynriah noh sha kiwei pat ki jaka. Ka jingshim jaka
ki shipai, CRPF, pulit la ki jaka rep ka la nang khate shuh shuh la
ka jaka rep. Tang ha Lum-Shyllong phra phew na ki shispah ka
jaka rep la shna la ka Head Quarter ki shipai, sah tang khyndiat ki
jaka ha Rngi Lum Shyllong ba ki nongrep ki loh ban pyndonkam.
la ki jaka rep ha Farm Upper Shillong, kumba hynriew phew na
ki shispah la shim ki shipai Air Force ban shna Head Quarter. Ha
Nongkrem ruh kumba mar shiteng ki jaka rep la shim ki Assam
Rifles ban shna Head Quarter. Ka long kumta ka rukom shim jaka
ki shipai ha Bhoi ruh bad ha Jaifitia.
Ka jingkurup jaka jong ki bar ri bad ki jylla ba marjan i'a ka
jaka rep bad kiwei kiwei ki jaka ba seisoh ka la nang pynrit shuh
ym tang la ka jaka rep, la ka ri Khasi Jaifitia ki baroh kawei. Ka
jingroi ka jaitbynriew bad ka jingshna i'a ki ling dew bilat kiba
heh ha mania ki shnong, ki dong bad ki lad ka la nang pynlong i'a
ka khyndew ban shu sah shrah, ym long shuh bad rep.
Ka jingtei la ki project bad ki karkhana ba pher ba pher ka
shu myntoi tang khyndiat ban ai kam i'a kito kiba la biang ka
jingpule katba ki nongrep bad ki khun samla ha ki nongkyndong
ki la shu shong kli kti bym loh jaka shuh ban rep bad bym don lad
ban trei da kiwei pat ki kam. Kiba bun ki khun samla na
nongkyndong, shynrang bad kynthei, ki wan tuid sha nongbah
Shillong bad kiwei ki nongbah ha ri Khasi Jaifitia ban wad kam,
bad lada kim ioh kam ka pynkut lad la ka jingim jong ki bad kaba
mih nangta ka saifidur da ka rukom pyrkhat kaba sniew bad thala

kat ban pynjynjar la ka ri bad ka jaitbynriew baroh kawei.
39
KATTO KATNE
KI JINGSHEM SHAPHANG KA REP
Kumba long kiwei pat ki rilum, karepka riang ka long kawei
na ki kam ba kongsan eh ha ri Khasi Jaifitia. Haduh mynta, baba
shim kyllum ki briew kiba im ha ka kam rep ki kham bun bun
shah ban ia ki briew kiba trei ia kiwei pat ki kam.
Haba kren shaphang ka jinglong ka mariang bad ka khyndew
ka shyiap, ka ri Khasi Jaifitia ka long ka ri kaba siesoh bad kaba
lah ban thung bun jait ki jynthung kynthup ia ki jhur bad ki soh
laiphew jait. Haba kren pat shaphang ki jaka kiba bit ba biang ban
rep ki duna haduh katta katta.
Ha ki phew snem kiba la leit noh, ki nongrep Khasi, haba ki
rep jhur ne kino kino ki jait jynthung ki pyndonkam da ka sboh
eit-masi, eit-kulai, eit-sniang bad kiwei kiwei ki jait sboh ba la
pynlong na u phlang bad ki niut ki flier. Ka khyndew ka sbur bha
bad ki jynthung rah ki seisoh shikatdei. Ym ju pynleh nohei ia ka
khyndew lada rep bun ne syllai ne kiwei kiwei ki rakom rep. Ki
jynthung kiba mih na ka khyndew ki long kiba bang bad shngiam
ban bam bad ki long rah kiba tei ia ka met ka phad. Ki briew ki
long kiba koit ba khiah krat bha, kiba don ka bor met kaba khlaifl,
ban kit ban bah, kiba lah ban trei ia ki kam kiba eh bad kajingpang
rah kam lah ban leh donbor ia ka jingim jong ki. Ki long ki briew
kiba trei shitom bad ki im shongprah shongshang.
Dang khyndiat snem naduh ba la sdang roi sted ka jingstad
saian, ki nongrep ha ri Khasi Jaifitia rah ki la sdang ban pyndonkam
ia ka sboh dawai, kum Anmonium Sulphate, Uria, Super phos-
phate, ki dawai pyniap kbfiiang bad kiwei kiwei ki jait dawai ba
pynmih thymmai na ka por sha ka por. Haba pyndonkam da kine

ki jait dawai, shipor ka khyndew ka seisoh bha kham bun shah
haba ia nujor bad ka rakom rep kaba pyndonkam da ka sboh kaba
mih na ki mrad. Katba nangmih ki snem ka jingpyndonkam ia ka
sboh dawai ka la nang pyntroifi ia ka khyndew bad ka jingpynmih
40
soh ka nang duna nang duna, ym stem, tang hapoh khyndiat sncm
ka ktayndew kaba seisoh ka la kylla ’dew shrah bad eh kum ka
’dew-ksang.
Kuniba long mynta bun bah ki jaka lum, ki pynthor, ki syllai
bad ki kper, kiba ju long mynno mynno kiba seisoh ki la shuh sah
shrah namar bym long shuh ban rep. Ki sung rep kba kiba kham
pawnam kum ka sung hapdeng ka Mawryngkneng bad Puriang,
ka Sung Mihmyntdu, ka Sung ha Baniun, ha Marbafiiang bad
Lyngkien, ha ’Wei-Lyngkut, ha Rangshken bad bun kiwei pat ki
sung rit ne pynthor ha ki thaift Jaintia bad Ri Bhoi ki la nangtroifi
nangtroin ka pynmih kba bad don ruh ki bym long shuh ban rep
kba. Ka daw jong kane ka long ka jingbymsngewthuh ban
pyndonkam ia ka sboh dawai ha ka rukotn kaba dei, ka jingtih
pathar ia u ’dewiong, ka jingtih shyiap bad ka jingpom pathar ia
ki khlaw. U phan bad u kba uba ju long u thicd snam uba bsa ia ka
jaitbynriew ha ri Khasi Jaifitia, mynta ki la nang jah rngai bym
long shuh ban rep, bad lada rep ruh, ym lah shuh ban pynmih ia
ka dor jong ka jinglut, nalor kata, ka jingtih pathar ia ka khyndew
bad u maw ha ki jaka lum bad ki them, haba wan u slap uba jur u
kit lut ia ka khyndew bad ka sboh baroh sha ri dkhar. Lada ngim
pyrshang ban iada na kane, ka ri jong ngi kaba ju long mynno
mynno kaba seisoh, shen kan sa kylla long ka ri shyiap.
Ha kine ki sngi haba ngi laid sha ki nongkyndong kylleng ka
ri Khasi Jaifitia ngi iohi ia ka jingeh ba ki khun samla jong ngi ki

ia kynduh. Kito kiba kyrshan ia ka jingim da ka rep ki la lyniiiar
bad kyrduh khait namar ka jingbym seisoh shuh ka rep, nalor
kata, ka jingpynlut haba thung bad ka jingmih na ka rep kim iadei
dor shuh, la kham bun ka jingpynlut ban ia ka jingioh. U phan
uba mynno mynno u ju long uba seisoh bad uba long u thied snam
bad ka jingkyrshan bah ia ka jingim ki nongrep, mynta u la kylla
long ka daw bah ban pyndap ram bad pynduk ia ki nongrep kiba
rep phan. Ka jingpynlut haba rep phan: ka bai thied symbai phan,
ka bai nongtrei, lea bai thied sboh bad dawai ban synreit ia u phan
41
haba i'apiong ka long kaba khraw shi katdei, katba ka dor u phan
ban die ha few ka long kaba tad haduh katta; kaba mih nangta ka
long ban pynbor ia ki nongrep ban iehnoh ka rep phan. Nalor
kata, u symbai phan ym ioh shuh uba thikna bad ka Sorkar ruh
lyngba ka Agriculture Department kam ai shuh ka jingkyrshan
kaba biang. Kumjuh ruh kito kiba ju rep kba ki la ngiah ban rep
namar ka bun ka jingpynlut ban ia ka jingi'oh. Kiwei pat kiba ju
trei khlaw ki la sdang sakma shane bad shawei pat namar kiba bun
ki khlaw ki la sdang synlen bad kito kiba im da ka bylla sngi
khamtam ha ka kam rep ki la lamwir namar uba duna ka jingrep,
duna ruh ka jingbylla.
Borabor, ki nongrep ki ud ba ka jingmih na ka rep ka long
kaba tad dor haduh katta katta. Bun ki nongrep ki ieh ia ka rep
namar kim lah ban pynmih wat tang ia ka bai trei ne bai lut. Haba
die ia u phan, lada na ka kti u nongrep ka long lai tyngka shi kilo,
ha ka kd ki nongdie lang kan long saw tyngka bad ha ka kti ki
nongdie khutia kan long hynriew tyngka shi kilo. U tympew, na
ka kti ki nongrep ka long san tyngka shi tap, ha ka kti ki nongdie

lang shiphew tyngka bad ha ka kti ki nongdie khutia katsan tyngka
bad kumta ter ter. Na ka kti ki nongrep ka mar ka long kaba tad
hynrei ha ka kti ki nongkhaii ka long kaba rem. Katba nang mih
ki snem, ki nongrep ki nang duk bad ki nongkhaii ki nang riewspah.
Lymda ka sorkar ka shimti ban thied ia ki mar rep lane kano kano
ka synjuk lang kan pyniaid la ka kam khaii, ki nongrep shimet
shimet kim lah ban kyrshan.
Ka jingkylli mynta ka mih ba kito kiba ju im da ka rep, haba
ym don rep shuh, kin leh aiu? Kito kiba ju bylla sngi ha ka kam
rep haba ym don rep shuh, shano kin leit? Kito kiba trei byla ha
ka kam khlaw, haba ym don khlw shuh, kin trei aiu? Ki samla
nongkyndong bad nongsor kumjuh khamtam kito kiba la pyndep
la ka jingpule ha ki skul, haba kim ioh kam sorkar ne kiwei pat ki
kam kin leh kumno? Kiba bun ki mih sa tang ki jingkylli. Bun ki
samla nongkyndong - shynrang bad kynthei, kim sngewtynnat
42
sbuh ban sbong ha nongkyndoog bad ki pyrshang ban wan tuid
kum ki sher-syngkai sha ki nongbah, lada ha SOf Shillong ne Jowai
ne Nongstoifi ne Nongpoh ne pynursla bad kiwei kiwei ki shnong
kiba lah ban ai kam, ban wad kam ban kyndai jakpoh, tang ban
im kam pher lada dei ban die ia ka kam die kotkhubor, kam die
waidong, kam driber ne helper, kam shna iing bad surok, kam
die jingdie ha rud surok bad kiwei kiwei ki kam rit kam ria.
Kiwei pat ki bym sngewtynnat ban trei ia kitei ki kam, ki bym
kwah ban trei shitom, ki shu sang makia bad ki saifldur da kawei
pat ka rokom pyrkhat ka bym i'adei satia bad ka Ipngung Iongseni
bad ka imlang sahlang. Ka jingkyrduh kam ka lah ban long kawei
na ki daw kaba wallam ka jingjyllei ki kam iakhalai, kam awria,
kam tuh, kam dih kiad bad kiwei kiwei ki kam sniew bad thala.

Donkam ia ka Sorkar bad ki Seng Bhalang ban iatrei lang

ban iada ia ki khun samla na ka jingkyrduh kam bad ki

KA JINGDON KI MAR HAPOH KHYNDEW
Ka ri Khasi Jaifitia ka long kawei na ki ri lum ha India kaba
riewspah namarkajingdonkimarbakDrdorhapohkhyndew. Nalor
ba ka long ka ri kaba seisoh, ka long ruh ka ri kaba U Blei u la
kyrkhu kyrpang da ka jingdon ki mar bakordor hapoh khyndew.
Da ki snem bad ki hajar snem kine ki mar ki don hapoh khyndew
hynrei dang khyndiat tylli ki snem ba ka jaitbynriew Khasi ka
shem bad ioh pyndonkam ia kine ki mar. Kiba kham kongsan eh
na kine ki mar ki long: U mawshun, ’dewiong, kaolin, siliminite,
dewbyrtha, copper, ksiar, lead bad zinc, qranite bad kiwei kiwei.
Nalor kine, ki ’riewstad wad mar poh khyndew, ki shem ia u
Uranium, u mar ba kordor eh ha kane ka juk mynta. La ong ba ki
mar hapoh khyndew tang ha ki thaifi Jaifitia ki long kumne harum:
(a) Mawshun - 402.00 million ton
(b) Dewiong 30.65.00 million ton
(k) Kaolin - 1.30 million ton
U Bah Sparbah Lyngdoh u ong ba kat kum ki geological
investigations la antad ba u mawshun u don haduh kumba 4000
(saw hajar) million ton ha ka jylla baroh kawei. (Khmih na ka kot
ka mariang bad ka spah jong ka p.32) bad u ong ruh ba kat kum
ka “Estimated Reserves” u dewiong ha ka jylla baroh kawei u don
kumba 530 (san spah laiphew) million ton. Tang ha ri Khasi Jaiiitia
u don kumba 170 (shispah hynfliew phew) million ton. Uba bun
eh u don ha Garo Hills.

Ia u mawshun la pyndonkam na ka bynta ban shna dewbilat
bad ia uba kham khuid la pyndonkam na ka bynta ban shna
‘Calcium Carbonate’ bad ‘Calcium Carbide’ bad kiwei kiwei
de ki jingpyndonkam. Kumba long mynta ia u mawshun la tih
45
kham bun na ka bynta ban pyniaid few sha Bangladesh bad kiwei
kiwei de ki jylla ka ri India. Ka jingtih mawshun kam i wailam
jingmyntoi -iei ia ka jaitbynriew namar ngim don ki karkhana
kiba biang ban ai kam ai jam ia ia ka jong ka jaitbynriew. Ka
Sorkar Jylla ka lah ban ioh katto katne ka jingmyntoi hynrei kaba
bun ka jingmyntoi ka Ioh ka kompani kaba pyntrei kam bad ki
nongtrei jong ka kiba ka wailam na bar ka jylla. Haba phai pat
shaphang ka jingpynjot 'ia ka lum ka wah, ka khlaw ka btap bad
ka khyndew ka shyiap ka ktah kumno ia ki trai khyndew, ka ri
bad jaitbynriew khamtam ia ki briew kiba shong shnong ha kito
ki jaka ba tih mawshun. Ka i'adei kumno kata ka jingong ban
pynneh ia ka khlaw ka btap, ka khyndew ka shyiap da ka jingtih
mawshun. Ym i don jingkyrmen shuh ban dang pynlong khlaw
ne ban rep jingrep pat ha ki jaka ba la khlong mawshun, kaba mih
nangta kito ki jaka kin shu sah shrah shi rta, ym lah ban pyndonkam
shuh ia ki.
Kumba long mynta ia u dewiong la tih ban phah (shallan) sha
ki jylla bapher bapher jong ka ri India bad Bangladesh. Ha ri
Khasi Jaintia ngi lah ban pyndonkam tang khyndiat eh ia u dewiong
namar ngim don ki karkhana kiba biang. Lah ban ong ba khyndai
phew khyndai na ka shi spah u dewiong uba till u leit shabar na ka
bynta ka jingdonkam kiwei pat ki jait bynriew.
Nalor u mawshun, u dewiong bad u sellimanite la don ka
jingpyrshang ban tih sa ki ’dew byrtha (kaolin), u Uranium bad
kiwei kiwei ki mar poh khyndew. Kan sa poi ka por ba ki briew

kin khlong lut ia ki mar baroh hapoh khyndew. Ynda ki briew ki
la ioh ban khlong lut ia ki mar poh khyndew kiba bun ki shnong
ha ri Khasi Jaintia kin sa kylla long tang ka jaka theh niut
namar bym long shuh ban shong ban sah, ban trei ban ktah
bad ki briew suki suki kin wan tuid ha ki nongbah bad
tyngngian. Kiba duk kin wan wad kam ban kyndoi jakpoh katba
kiba ’riewspah kiba la dep kynshew spah bad tei ling ha nongbah
kin shongthait bad im pahuh pahai.
46
Kawei pat ka jingma kaba ap ia ka ri bad ka jaitbynriew ka
long ka jingbym sngewthuh ia ka rukom dh (system) ia u mawshun,
dewiong bad kiwei kiwei ki mar poh khyndew ha ka rukom kaba
dei bad kaba biang. Don kiba tih ia ka khyndew, haba kim shem
’dewiong, ki lehnoh khlem tap; kiwei pat, ki tih bad ioh ban
khlong katto katne u ’dewiong, ki khlem tep na tap ia ka khyndew;
sa kawei pat, ki tih bad ngam jylliew haduh ban da ioh khlong lut
ia u ’dewiong hynrei kim tep ia ka khyndew bad ka shu sah duh
ka jinglong thliew. Nalor kata, ia u ’dewiong uba mih na thliew,
haba u pra ha madan madiah, la shu ieh noh bad khlem khar; ka
rukom lum thup ka long kat haba lap bad shem ka jaka kaba kham
suk ban rah bad kit, kam pher la ka long ka jaka khlaw ne harud
surok ne ha phyllaw i'ing. Suki suki katba nangmih ka snem don
ki thliew ’dewiong kaba dambit noh hynrei kiba kham bun, ki
thliew ki nangheh nangiar bad jylliew na ka jingkhlong u slap; u
’dewiong u pra kylleng kylleng na ka jaka lum thup bad ka um
’dewiong ka tuid sha ki phudwah bad ngam ruh ha khyndew;
kaba mih nangta, iap lut u phlang u kynbat, u fliut u flier, ki dieng
ki siej, ki puit ki khfiiang bad ki doh ki dohkha ha ki wah; shuh

shuh ka pynsniew lut ia ka um ha ki wahduid ne ki wahbah bad ka
um bam umdih. Ki briew ki lah ban kmen ba ki ioh kyrhai u
kpu ban bam na ka spah ’dewiong te hynrei kim dei ban klet
ba kan poi ka por ba kim lah ban nguid ia u kpu lada kim
ioh ka um ban dih. Ka jingtih pathar bad jingbym suitfiiew ban
lum bad kynshew ia ki marpoh khyndew ha ka rukom kaba dei
kan pynjot lut ia ki jaka puta, ki jaka rep, ka khlaw ka btap,
ki pynthor kba, ki dieng ki siej bad ka pynsniew dur ia ki lum
ki wah.
Haba ngi kren ia ki mar hapoh khyndew khamtam u dewiong
bad u mawshun, ka jingphah shalan shabar ri ka long kaba jur. La
ngim ioh ka jingkheifl kaba biang, la antad ba ka jingmih kan
long da ka spah klur shisnem shisnem. La kheifi antad ba ka jinglut
kan long kumne: ka bai nongtrei kan long kumba laiphew na ka
shispah - kiba bun ki nongtrei ki dei ki nongwei bad ki mynder
47
ri; ka bai kit kan long kumba laiphew na ka shispah - kaba khain
bun ki nongfiiah kali, ki dei ki nongwei bad ki truck ruh ka jong
ki nongwei; kumba arphew na ka shispah, ka leit sha ki nongkhaii
- kaba kham bun ki nongkhaii ruh ki dei ki nongwei; lehse tang
kumba arphew na ka shispah ka sah ha ki trai ri bad trai khyndew.
Ka jingmyntoi la ki trai ri ka long tang khyndiat katba ka jingpynjot
ia ki lum ki wah, ki shnong ki thaw, ki ling ki sem bad ka
longbriew manbriew ka long kaba shyrkhei. Tip katno snem ki
briew ki lah ban shong bad im ha kito ki shnong kiba la pynjulor
bad pynjot da ka jingtih ia ki mar hapoh khyndew. Kiba riewspah
ki lah ban phet bad riehtngen ha ki nongbah hynrei kiba duk ki
lah ban kylla nongphetwir (refugee) ha la ka jong ka ri. Nalor

kata, ka jingjyllei ki nongwei bad ki mynder ri ka ap ka jingma
kaba khraw ia ka ri bad ka jaitbynriew.
Uwei u professor na ka thaifi Jaifitia u bynfliaw da kaba ong
ba ka jingwan kynsan ka spah ’dewiong ka pynngop ia ki khun
samla ha ki jingsngewbha laiphew rukom bad kam thala laiphew
jait. U ong ruh, bun ki khun samla kirn nang shuh ban iiiewkor ia
ka kam ka jam, kim mon shuh ban trei ban ktah bad ki shu pynlut
kai ia ka por bad pynsyrwa kai ia ka spah ’dewiong.
Ka jingma kaba kham kongsan kaba ap mynta ka long ka
jingwan tuid kynsan ki jaitbynriew kiba pher bapher - ki riewlum,
ki nongwei bad ki mynder ri na kylleng ka ri India bad nabar ri
ruh ban wan shong basa ha Sor Shillong, ha ki happud ki ri Khasi
Jaifitia bad ha ki jaka pdeng jong ka ri khamtam ha ki jaka ba jur
ka tih dewiong, tih mawshun, tih surok bad ki jaka ba don ki
karkhana. Ka ri jong ngi ka long da shisha ka ri kaba rit, kaba
seisoh kaba riewspah bad ka jaitbynriew kaba tang shitroh. Ngi
sheptieng ba kine ki jaitbynriew kiba wan shongbasa kin sa
kylla long trai ri bad ba ma ngi kiba long ki trai ri ngjn sa
kylla long ki mynder ri. Ka jingiai kylla ka jingsynshar ka bub
borabor ia ki rit paid ha ka jingeh bad jingma.
48
Kumba la long mynta, ka jaka shong jaka sah ki la nang duna
la jan man la ki shnong. Ka jaka rep ka la nang sab shrah bad
nangduh, ki wah ki la nang tyrkhong, ki umpohliew ki la mgat
lut bad sa tang katto katne por bun ki shnong kin nym 'ioh shuh
wat tang ka um dih ruh. Ka jingpom pathar ia ki khlaw bad ka
jingtih jhulum ia ki mar pob khyndew ka la pynsniew lut ia ka ri
bad ka jaitbynriew. Kane ka pateng bynriew mynta i kumba kan

bam duh nob baroh ia ka spah kaba U Blei u la buh ha sla bad
hapoh khyndew. Hato ngi ju pyrkhat ia ka apot ka pateng bynriew
Khasi ka ban wan ?
KA JINGTIH IA U URANIUM
Ha ka report “U Mawphor” dated 6 th August, 2007 U
Dr. H. Lyngdoh, diploma in Radiography u ong, ka radiation
jong u Uranium uba don ha ka par-Uranium ne hangno hangno ka
ksam lyngba bad rung hi ne ka kynthah (exposed) ia u briew bad
ka ktah lut baroh shi rynieng khamtam haba ring mynsiem ia kata
ka Iyer bih ne haba ioh rung ki pui pui na ka khyndew Uranium
wat lada dei tang ka ’dew kyntait ne ka eit-Uranium ka don ka
jingsniew.
Ka Uranium Radiation lada kren tang shi kyntien ka long
kaba ‘Triem’ palat mar iabud bad ka jingiap hi.
Ka jingpynshai ka Khasi Students Union ha U Mawphor dated
6 th June, 2007 halor ka jingma na kaba tih ia u Uranium ka long
kumne:
Ka KSU lyngba kane ka kyrwoh ka kwah ban pynpaw ha phi
ia ki jingma kiba ap ia ka Jaitbynriew jong ngi na ka daw ba ka
Sorkar India, lyngba ka Department of Atomic Energy (DAE),
ka Atomic Minerals Division (AMD) bad ka Uranium Corpora-
tion of India Limited (UCIL), ka kwah ban tih ia u Uranium uba
don ha ka kane ka ri jong ngi. Kane ka jingpyrshang ka Ia sdang
naduh ka snem 1990 tangba ka la kynjah noh namar ka jingpyrshah
49
kaba jur da uba bun uba Iang. Sa shisien pat, ha kine ki ar snem
lai snem ba la leit, kite! ki kompeni haneng ki la pyrshang biang
ban ioh lad ban tih i'a u Uranium. Mynta lei lei ka jingpyrshang
ka la long kaba tyngeh namar ba ki la ioh ia ka jingkyrshan na ki
katto katne ki khwan myntoi, bad khamtam na ka Khasi Hills
Autonomous District Council (KHADC) kaba, ha ka jaka ban
iada ia ka khyndew ka shyiap bad ka hok longtrai jong ki trai ri

trai muluk, ka la kylla long pynban ka agent die khyndew na ka
bynta kitei ki kompeni pukni bam don pyut, kiba kwah ban khfliot
bad thombor ia ngi.
Ki jingshisha shaphang u Uranium
U Uranium u dei u marpoh khyndew uba khia tarn na ki mar
poh khyndew baroh. U don ha ki bynta ba jylliew ka mei ramew
bad ym don ba lah ban iohi lane ktah ia u lada ym tih da ki phew
lane spah miter shapoh.
U Dr. Elias Carreno-Peralta, u riewsaid-aifi na Peru, South
America u ong: “ha ka District Mcusani, la antad ba don u Uranium
haduh 200,000 ton. Ha ka ktien ki Incas (ki trai muluk ha Peru)
ia u Uranium la khot kyrteng “ayacachi" kaba mut u “mluh ka
jingiap” lane “ka mluh kaba pyniap”.
Kumno kan ktah ia ka Jaitbynriew ?
Ka jingtih Uranium ha kiwei ki Ri ka pyrthei bad ha ka jylla
Jharkhand ha India ki la pynpaw ia shibun ki jingma bad ki
jingsniew ba shyrkhei ba ki paidbah ki la hap ban mad. Kine
harum ki long katto katne ki nuksa:
a) Ha ka shnong Yucca ha America ki la sdang tih Uranium
naduh ka snem 1947 bad ha ka snem 1984 la lap ba 86,000
ngut ki nongtrei ki la ioh pang na kiba bun jait ki jingpang
kiba kynthup ia ka Cancer Snam (Leukeamia), ka jingkylla
rit ne jah noh ki dohjem (Impotency), lyngki (Sterility), ka
pang ring (Epilepsy) bad ka pang bampong (Cancer). Ha
kaba khatduh la hap ban pynkynriah noh ia kine ki nongtrei
shawei pat ban sumar ia ki.
50
b) Ha ka snem 1962, ha South Dakota, ka jaka jong ki Red
Indian, la tih la u Ur anium da ka Anaconda Company. Bad
ha ka snem 1979, ha katei hi ka jaka, la kheifi ha haduh 70%
ki khyllung kiba dang kha kim lah pynhiar mynsiem ba khim
eh ki tor bad 38% ki kynthei pat ki hap khun bad khalor.
k) Ha ka snem 1985, ha Jadugoda ha Jharkhand (kaba hap

mynshwa ha ka jylla Bihar), namar ka jingtih Uranium ka
UCIL, la lap ba ki jait Ho, Santal bad jait Munda ki la kem
pang na kita ki saw jait ki jingpang ba la jer haneng bad bun
ruh ki kynthei ki la khalor bad kha khun anna.
d) Ki Aborigines, kiba dei ki trai ri ka Ri Australia, ki la
iakynduh ia shibun ki jingeh la ka long na ka jingpang
jingshitom ne ka jingduh noh ia ka khyndew ka shyiap, ha
kaba ki la shah pynmih noh na la ki shnong la jong, tang na
ka daw ba ka sorkar Australia ka la tih Uranium na ka jaka
jong ki
Haba ngi la iohi la kine kiei kiei, ka la pynkmg ia ngi
ban ngin pyrshah ia kane ka jingthmu kaba smew halor
kine ki nongrim -
1 . Ka jingktah ia ka koit ka khiah - Ki stad scientist kylleng
ka pyrthei ki la pynshisha ba ka jingtih Uranium ka wanrah
ia ka jingktah bashyrkhei ia ka koit ka khiah kynthup lang ia
ki briew, ki mrad, ki sim ki doh bad baroh ki jingthaw kiba
don sawdong, la ki long ki dieng ki siej, ki jingthung jingtep,
ka urn ka wah ne ka mei mariang hi baroh kawei. Kine baroh
ki jia, namar lada sei ia u Uranium shabar, ki mih ki phngiat
bad ki kjit jingshai ba long bih (radioactive rays), kiba don
lai jait. Kine ki kynthup ia ki phngiat jingshai basniew bha
kum ki alpha ray, ki beta ray bad ki gama ray.
Ki alpha ray ki dei kita ki phngiat jingshai long bih kiba lah
ban her jan na sla khyndew bad lada ki dei ha ki briew ne
mrad, ki dambit ha ka snieh doh bad nangta ki sa rung lyngba
51
ki thliew syep ne ki thliew shfiiuh ban ngam sha ka snam.
Dei da kumta ba ki briew ne ki mrad ki sa loh pang Cancer
Snam (Leukeamia).
Ki beta ray pat ki kham her jngai khyndiat bad ki trei kam
kumjuh.

Ki gama ray ki long kiba lah ban her jngai tam bad lada ki
dei ha ka met kino kino ki kynja ba im, ki sam haduh shiphut
shiteng shapoh bad ki sa sahkut sah hapoh ki ksah. Kito ki pfangiat
jingshai kiba her shabar pat ki lah ban her haduh 300 km bad ka
jingtrei kam jong ki ha ka dur ki jingpang bapber, la ha ki briew
ne ki kynja ba im baroh, ka lah ban hiar pateng la pateng haduh
80,000 snem.
Shuh shuh, na ka jingtih bad jingtai ia u Uranium ka mih
ruh ka Iyer bih kaba ki khot ka Radon Gas. Kane ka Iyer bih,
namar ba ka kham khia ban ia ka Iyer, ka par najan ka sla khyndew
bad ka lah ban phriang da ki spah kilo mitar wat lada don tang ka
Iyer kaba beh malu mala. Lada ring mynsiem ia kane ka Iyer bih
yn ioh ia ka jingpang bampong jong ki tor (Lung Cancer) bad
kiwei kiwei ki jingpang tor (lung-related diseases).
2) Ka jingwan Buhai Shnong kiwei ki Jaitbynriew - Ha
Jadugoda, ka jaka ba tih Uranium ka UCIL, da ki hajar ngut
ki nongtrei ba nabar ki la wan ban trei bad buhai shong ha
katei ka ja'ka. Kum kane ka jingjia kan wan jia kumjuh ruh
ha ka ri jong ngi lada ngi ailad ia kitei ki kompeni ban wan
tih Uranium, bad kane kan ktah ia ka imlang sahlang bad ia
ka snap (identity) ba kyrpang jong ngi, bad khlem pep ngin
sa kylla long ki rit paid ha la ka jong ka ri.
3) Ka jingduh ia ki Jaka - Lada ngi ailad ia ka tih Uranium,
da ki hajar akar ki jaka ngin duh ei. Ka UCIL ha kaba
nyngkong ka kwah ban shim sbwa 748 hectar (Ha) lane 1848
Acre bad tang ka pung ban bret jakhlia sha poh (tailings pond)
ka long 200 hectare (Ha) lane 492 Acre. Hynrei mynta pat.
52
lchnang ba nym pynpaw palat ia kata ka jingdonkam jaka

kaba shyrkhei, ka UCIL ka lah shu pynduna ia ka jingkwah
shim jaka sah 351 hectare (Ha) lane 867 Acre. Wat kane ruh
ka la long ka jingdonkam jaka kaba heh bad kaba lah thikna
ka long ba ka UCIL kan long thik kum ka jing'iathuh khana
shaphang ka ud bad u kynrad - ha kaba nyngkong ka ud ka
khroh ia u kynrad ban ailad la ka ban rung tang ka khlieh ha
sem namar ba khriat eh, bad suki suki lyngba ka jingkhroh
bad jingpynbiej ka la ioh ruh baroh kawei ka met bad ha kaba
khatduh ka kynjat syndon ia u trai shabar ka sem. Hato, ngi
lah mo ban shu ailad 'ia ki jaka jong ngi ban iai duh na kawei
ka por sha kawei pat 7
4) Ka jingkylla Len Shipai - Ki kompeni tih Uranium kin
wan rah ryngkat bad ki la ki shipai ki swar ki ban ap pahara
ia kine ki par jong ki. Ka ri jong ngi ka la long lypa ka ri
kaba la shah pynsohsat laiphew jait ha ka jingjyllei palat ki
len shipai. Kumta ka jingkylla len shipai ki par Uranium kan
nang pynkordit shuh shuh ia ki trai shnong nalor ba kan
pynlong ia ki kiba rit bor bad rit mynsiem kum ki khnai.
Ka jingpynshai ka KWWADA ha U Mawphor dated
11"’ June, 2007 kaba ong “ym dei ban tih ia u Uranium ka long
kumne” :
Ki lum chota Nagpur (Jharkhand) jong ka jylla Bihar ka dei
ka jaka shong bad ka bri jong ki riewlum Santhals, Hos, Gonds
bad Mundas. Ha kane ka thaifi don ki par Uranium ha Jadugoda,
ha Batin bad ha Nawpahar ia kiba la tih da ka Uranium Corpora-
tion of India Ltd. (UCIL). Kine ki par ki don antad kumba 2000
phut shapoh khyndew. Hajan kitei Id par don ka karkhana kyrsih,
sait bad phrang ia u Uranium ba dang tih. Ia ki lyngkhot lyngkhai
Uranium bad ki eit Uranium ba la kyntait na katei ka karkhana la
pyntuid lyngba ki shnong ba markhap da ki tyndong nar (pipes).

La lum bad la ieh ia ki ha ka pung eit Uranium kaba la tei ha
kawei ka them ba iar jong ka shnong Telaitan bad Dhunurduh.
53
Kane ka them rep kba kaba seisoh bha mynshuwa ka la kylla long
pat ka them ieh shrah kaba don bih mynta.
Ka urn bad ka khyndew kaba don ha katei ka them ka la long
bih lut. Kane ka bih ding ka bam pong suki suki i'a ki dkhot met
jong ki briew, ki mrad bad ki jingthung jingtep kiba don ha katei
ka them bad ki shnong ba marjan. Ka jingpang kynrei lai phew
rukom ka wan urlong ha ki trai shnong. Kine ki jingpang ki

ka i'ai bteng bad hiar-pateng sha ki pateng kiba hadien rah.
Mynta, yn phai pat sha ka jingpyntrei kam ia ki par Uranium
ha kitei ki jaka. Ka UCIL ha Jadugoda, Batin bad Nawpahar ka
don kumba 4,000 ngut ki nongtrei ba thikna bad kumba 1,000
ngut ki nongtrei bylla ia kiba shu khot tang ha ki por ba donkam.
Na kitei ki 4,000 ngut ki nongtrei ba thikna, kiba bun ki dei ki
nongtih ha ki par bad kham khyndiat ngut kiba trei ha ka kyrdan
pyniaid kam halor ka office bad ha Hospital. Na ki nongtih ha ki
par, kumba 95 na ka 100 ki dei ki riewlum trai muluk. Hynrei ha
baroh ki kam pyniaid kiba halor, ym don trai muluk satia. La
pyniaid mon da ki riewstad kot na shabar. Ki bor pyniaid ki set
buhrieh, ia baroh ki jingma kiba don ha ka bad kiba mih na u
Uranium na ki nongtih par bad na ka pyrthei, katkum ka Atomic
Energy Act jong ka snem 1962. ki buhrieh ia ki jingma bad ki
jingmynsaw kiba mih ha ka jingtih, ka jingsiat bad ka jingphriang

ia u na kaba lui lui bad ki bym tip bym bha.

Jia ryngkat ruh ba u Uranium u don ha ka meiramew jong ka
Ri Khasi Jaiflda. La tip shai ba u don ha Domiasiat, Wahkaliar,

poh khyndew ha Domiasiat la pyrshang mushlia da ki stad saian
na Delhi, Mumbai bad Hyderabad ha kaba ki la dep sei katto
katne. Mynta pat la don ka jingthmu ban tih lut syndon ia u

pyniaid par bad karkhana sait Uranium yn pyniaid da ki stad ki
Jhad bar jylla; ym pat don ki stad Khasi.
Haba la tip shai ia kiei kiei kiba la urlong, ha Jadugoda, batin
bad nawpahar (ha ki lum Chotta Nagpur) bad kumjuh ruh ha
Three Mile Island (USA) bad ka Chernobyl (Ukraine) ka long
kaba sheptieng ba kitei ki juh ki apot kin wan urlong, ha ri Khasi
Jaifitia. Lada jia ba ka jingmushlia ia u Uranium kan wan urlong
Id khun ki ksiew Khasi ki pang kin khrew bad kin lyngkot rta.
Ynda haba ki stad saian na shabar ki la dep tih lut (lada lah) bad
ieh ia ki lyngkhot lyngkhai Uranium ha ryngkew, ki lum ki wah
kin sah shrah (bym long bad lah shuh ban rep ban riang). Ki jaka
Id puta ki Khasi kin sa nangrit bad nangjot. Shano ka lawei ka
jong ki. U Uranium na Ri Khasi Jaifitia un sa pyqjot ym tang
Sa ki Khasi, hynrei yn thang Sa ka pyrthei ruh.

jfa u Uranium dei ban ieh shapoh mei ramew kaba la buh

Ka jingpynshai u Bah K.M Khonglah ha U Mawphor,
22® Risaw (October), 2005 ka long kumne ;
Ka jingshah pynbor (presurise) ban khlong noh i'a u mar ba
rem dor bha ha ka pyrthei, u Uranium, ka la wan. Une u mar poh
khyndew u lah ban pynmih i'a ka hording electric bad u lah rah
ban pynduna i'a shiteng ka pyrthei. “Shi kilo u Uranium u
iaryngkat bad 22,000 ton u dewi'ong” la dep tip ba don u
Uranium naduh ka snem 1972 hynrei la rai ba u dang dei ban sah
shapoh niamra. Lada lap ba u don ha sla khyndew, dei ban tep
shapoh. Lada khynfiiuh jumai, u Uranium u mih bad kiew sha sla
pyrthei bad dei ban pyntip mar mar sha ki bor wat shaduh UN ba

kan wan ban pynkhuid i'a u dew stem ne waste (jakhlia) khnang
ban iada ia ki nongshong shnong bad i'a ki khlaw ki btap, ka
umbam ka umdih, ka , Mariang.
U Uranium u don 10 million ton tang ha Domiasiat bad bun
phew million ton ha baroh kawei ka South Khasi & Jaifltia Hills.
La lap ruh i'a utei u jait mar ha East Garo Hills. U Prime Minister
ka India, u Man Mohan Singh, dang ha une u bnai September u
la kren paidbah ha New York, U SA i'a ka jingdon bun u Uranium
(kumba 80 mn ton) ha Meghalaya/NE India ka BBC (world) bad
ka CNN (World ka la rah i'a ka sur jingkren jong u sha baroh
kawei ka pyrthei. Ki khmat na kylleng ki ri bapher bapher ki la
peit khliaw kumno ban 'ioh khlong ia utei u mar shna Atom bomb
na Meghalaya, India bad kin da invest ruh da ki spah hajar klur
tyngka. Ka jingiaknieh ia utei u jait mar ba rem dor ka dm ha
baroh kawei ka pyrthei. Ha ki ri ba la shai ha USA ha ki jaka jaka
ha Europe ka jingkhang ban rung briew/paidbah ha Id jaka ba don
ka jingdh bad jingshna ne said plutonium (Nuclear Plant) ka long
haduh 35 kilo metre sawdong ne 35 sq km. Ka dor ba la tyrwa la
56
ka West Khasi Hills ban shna bad till crude Uranium ka long 1200
klur tyngka. Mynta naduh ba la pynbna paidbah sha ka pyrthei na
New York, kajingiakhlieh dor kan nang kiew bad kan ym duna ia
ka 4, 800 klur, kata saw shah. Naloru Uranium don sa u Mawshun
u bym lut shuh uba ki briew na ri thor ki lyngngoh ba ngi ia siang
surok da ki mawshun ba remdor. U Dewiong u bun kyrhai bad la
kheifl ba yn lut noh ha ka snem 2020 A.D. ia u dewiong ha
khliehriat la buh ba un lut ynda tih 15 snem lynter. La dep tih
mynta la 9 snem lynter. Ki trai khyndew ki la iohnong bha kat

kum ka hok ba ki dei ban ioh bad kiba na bar jylla ruh ki la ia
longbriew bha. Ka jingbym don ki karkhana saifi rong surok bad
kiwei kiwei kiba i'aid da u dewiong ka la pynsyrwa ei ia ka
jingiohnong ba dei ban ioh shibun. Kiba wan khaii pat na shabar
kim da salia eh haduh katta katta ia ka jingpynkhuid pat ia ka
mariang bad ki um bam um dih. Na ki waste (jakhlia) ba shu ieh
kumto, kin sa mih ki briew ne jaitbynriew ba pang khrew bad kan
ktah la kumno kumno haduh saw pateng bynriew. Kane ka long
kat kum na ki warnings ba ju ai ki World Health Centres ha kylleng
ka pyrthei.
Ki briew jong ngi kim pat mlien ha ki kam karkhana bad ka
Meghalaya ha ka jingshisha kam dei ka Industrial State bad kiba
daiig im ha ka rep ka riang ka long 80/100 (phra phew na ka
shispah). La shu tih ia ki crude ne raw materials bad gain sha
kiwei pat ki jaka ne ki ri. Ban hikai ia la ki jong ki briew ha ki
kam ba thymmai ba iadei bad kitei ki spah ramew, donkam ban
pynshlur bad ban ai training kumba la ju leh ha kiwei kiwei ki
jylla. (ka jingfap thngan ha bun ki ri ha Africa ka dd eh na
ka jingbymnang shuh ki pateng briew ban leit phai sha ka
rep ka riang na ka jingtih ksiar bad mawlynnai ha ki 250
snon ba la leit ha ki por ba synshar ki phareng). U nar ha Ri
Khasi ba la jan lut bad ka ksiar ki shong ha ki syrtap ba kham
jylliew kum ha Mawphlang bad Mairang bad ban tih ia ki ka
jmglut kan kham bun ban ia ki jingniih.

Ka Meghalaya ka long ka jylla kaba riewspah bha ha ka spah

Ha ka long run, ngi dei ban kynmaw ia ki kyntien tynunen

rilum jong ngi! Ngin ym die duh ha ki mynder shisngi.”
Shuwa ban tih lut ia ki mar poh ramew, dei ban seng ki
karkhana ba radbah ha la ri.
Jingkynthoh :
Ka Ri Khasi Jaifitia ka long kawei na ki ri lum kaba riewspah
ha ka jingdon ki mar ha sla bad hapoh khyndew. U Blei u la
kyrkhu dap biang da ki jingdon ki mar bad ki jingthaw ba im
laiphewjait ha sla khyndew bad hapoh um. Da shisha u la kyrkhu
ia ka jaitbynriew Khasi hynrei ngi khot hi ia ki jingeh, ki
jingpang, ki jingjyqjar bad jingiap hi. Ki kpa tymmen jong
ngi ki long ki briew kiba im suk im said lyngngai la ka rep ka
riang, ka trei ka ktah, ka khaii pateng ha la ka jong ka ri bad
shabar ruh. Ki pyndonkam hok ia ka spah kaba buh U Blei ryngkat
bad ka trei shitom da kaba pynmih syep ban pynim ia lade bad ia
ka iing. Ka long ki briew kiba shait ba khlaifi, kiba im shongprah
shongshang ki bam ka jingbam kaba khuid kaba long ha sla bad
58

hapoh khyndew, kim ju mad ia ka jingpang kaba eh bad kaba
sniew bad kim pat sngewthuh ruh ban pyndonkam ia ka dawai.
Ha kane ka juk mynta baroh ki la jia long kiba phylla bad
kiba kylla bad ki briew kim ‘ioh shuh ban im suk im saifl kumba
long ki kpa tymmen mynshwa. Ha kane ka juk ka jingkylla, ki
briew kim mon shuh ban rep ban riang, ban kit ban bah, kim
kwah shuh bad trei shitom khamtam da ka bor met, kynjai ki
pynsuk ia lade da kaba khura lut ia ka spah mariang kaba U Blei u
la buh ha sla bad poh khyndew. Tang ban ioh ban im pahuh bad
pynsngewbha i'a lade kim salia ban pynsyrwa ia ka spah kaba la
kynshew ha ka mariang bad kim patiaw ruh ban pyrkhat ia ka
lawei jong ka pateng bynriew ka ban wan.
Kumba long mynta, ki jingpang kiba sniew bad ki jingthala
kiba har rukom ki la nang kha roi ha ka jaitbynriew bad ka ri hi
baroh kawei. Ki briew kim pyrkhat ba ka jingpang ka la nang
jyllei na ka por sha ka por ka long, lehse, na ka daw jong ki
jingbam, ka um, ka Iyer bad ka jingwan mid ki briew kiba kit
jingpang na ki jylla bapher bapher ka ri India ban wan trei ha
kane ka ri jong ngi. Nalor kata, ki mih ki report kiba pher bapher
na ki Seng Bhalang, ne ki kot khubor bad kiwei kiwei shaphang
ka Iyer bih kaba mih na ka jingtih ’dewiong, mawshun. Uranium,
Shyiap bad kiwei kiwei ki mar poh khyndew hynrei ki briew kiba
kamai spah na ki mar poh khyndew kim da salia eh ia kata. Ka
jingtih ia ki mar poh khyndew ka la pynsniew lut ia ki um, ka
Iyer, ki jaka rep, ki lum ki wah bad kiwei kiwei namar ka jingtih
pathar bad buh kulmar ia ki marpoh khyndew kat haba lap ba
shem wat haduh phyllaw iing hi. Ka jingpang ka mih ym tang ha

ki jaka marpoh khyndew ka mih ruh ha ki shnong bad ki nongbah
hi kiba dei iaid ki kali kiba kit ia kine ki mar. Ka ri jong ngi kaba
ju long mynno mynno ka bym ju don jingpang, mynta ki la jyllei
da ki jingpang kiba pher bapher bad ldba sniew haduh kiba bun ki
briew ha ka ri jong ngi ki hap ban krih kylleng kylleng ka ri India
ban wad jingsumar namar bym lah ban sumar ha la ka jong ka
59
jylla. Ki ioh ka spah na kaba tih ia ki marpoh khyndew hynrei
kiba bun ki briew ki shu lut noh kata ka spah tang ha kaba shu
shah sumar pang, nalor kata, sa ka jingkhrew ka met bad ka
jingswaikajingim.
Ha ka bynta ba shakhmat ha ki kot khubor la kdew shai ba ki
Company bar Jylla ki don jingthmu ban khlong lut ia u Uranium
uba don ha ri Khasi Jaifltia bad la kdew ruh ‘ia ki jait jingpang kiba
lah ban mih na ka jingtih ia u Uranium. U Uranium hi u long
uwei na ki mar poh khyndew uba lah ban pynmih bun jait ki jait
jingpang kiba sniew (bym lah ban pynkoit) kham sniew ban ia
kino kino ki mar poh khyndew. Ha ki thaifl Jaifltia kiba bun ki
um, ki wah, ki jaka lum bad jaka rep ki la sniew lut na ka daw ka
jingtih ’dewiong bad Mawshun. Lada tih sa ia u Uranium ha ka
thaifi Sepngi ka ri Khasi, kumno kan long ka ri lum Khasi Jaifltia.
Ka ri jong ngi ka la long lypa ka jylla ri lum kaba rit kaba duna ka
jaka rep, jaka shong bad lada khlong lut ia ki mar poh khyndew
ka ri kan shu long tang ka ri khyndew ne ri shyiang bad ki
nongkyndong ki shu ialum lut kum u ksaifi ha ki nongbah. Kaba
sheptieng eh ka long ba ka ri Khasi-Jaifltia ka ju long mynno
mynno naduh ki kpa kulong jong ngi ka ri kaba pyngngad, kaba

dap da ki um kiba shngiam ki Iyer kiba suh sieng bad ki lum kiba
pyngngad te hynrei ha ka shi khyllipmat kan sa long noh ka ri
kaba dap da ka jingpang, jingkhrew bad ring swai. Ngi i'ohi ia ka
roi ka pa haba tih ia ki mar poh khyndew te hynrei ngim iohi pat
ia ka jingnang ring swai ka ri bad ka jaitbynriew. Ka jingtih ia
ki mar poh khyndew kam da long eh ka jingmyntoi ia ka ri
Khasi-Jaifltia, ka long pynban tang ka jingpynjot bad
pynsniew dur ia ka ri bad kaba pynkha roi ia ki jingpang har
nikom. Kan jia ka por ynda la lut u Uranium, kane ka jaka
bad ki jaka ba marjan da ki phew hajar acre yn ym long
shuh ban shong ban sah ban rep ban riang, kan long ka jaka
shong ki khfiiang jingpang bad ki sna-iap (ghost land).

SHYIAP NA KI WAH BAD TIH SHYIAP BAD MAW
NA KI LUM :
Tang kiba lah ban kdew hangne ki long :
1. Baniun - Mylliem - Umtyngar bad ki jaka ba markhap.
2. Laitkor - Pomlakrai - Umiew. 3. Thaifi Wahkhen bad
Lad Mawreng - Kreit. 4. Thaifl Umrynjah bad ki jaka ba

markhap. 5. Lyngkyrdem - Siatbakon. 6. Thaifi Umsyiem -
Umkrem - Wah Rew - Wah Khasmar - ki jaka Khasi hynrei
la tih shyiap bad mar da ki khar Bangla. 7. Ki thaifl Wah
kynshi bad ki jaka ba markhap. 8. Ki thaifl Nongstoifl bad ki
jaka ba markhap.
61
Don bun kiwei pat ki jaka ha ri Khasi Jaifitia kiba ki briew ki
khlong maw bad shyiap tangba ym la ban fiiew lut hangne.
Ki briew ki khlong lut i’a u Shyiap, u maw bad ki khyndew
na ka wah , ki rud wah bad na ki lum da kaba ai daw ba ki hap ban
trei na ka bynta kan kyndoi jakpoh. Ki rud wah, ki lum kiba ju
long mynno mynno ki jaka ba ju rep pateng la pateng bad kiba
seisoh, mynta ki long ki par shyiap par maw. Ka jingrep ka long
ka jingtrei kaba pyn-im pateng-la-pateng ia la ka jong ka i'ing, ka
ri bad ka jaitbynriew. Ka khyndew ka shyiap ka long ka jingai ba
kordor U Blei ba u buh ba u briew kin rep kin riang bad im shi
junom. Haba ki briew kirn mon shuh ban pynshitom ia lade ban
rep ban riang hynrei ki khlong pynban ia ka khyndew, u shyiap
kaba dei ban rep ban riang bad bam duh noh 1a ka jingbuh U Blei,
hato haba kane ka jingbuh U Blei kan lut noh, sa da ei pat kin trei
ban pynim ia lade, ia ka ri bad ka jaitbynriew. U Blei u buh ia ki
mrad, ki sim bneng, ki dohkha bad ka khyndew ka shyiap ba kin
pynroi pynsan bad pynim ia lade da ki, hynrei haba ki bam duh
noh ia ki da ei pat kin pynim i'a lade ha ka pateng ka ban wan. Ha
kane ka juk mynta ki briew ki i’alch thma ha lade shimet shiemt
tang kumno ban im. Ha ka jingshisha, ki la dei ban trei shitom
bad wad da ki lad baroh, ym ban bam duh noh ia ki jingdon jingem

baroh katba don ha ka mariang khamtam ia ki jingbuh U Blei.
KI MAWBYNNA BAD KIWEI PAT KI JAIT MAW KIBA
KHAM KONGSAN
Ki mawbynna ki don ha ki bun ki jaka bad ki shnong bapher
bapher ka ri Khasi Jaifitia. Ki longshwa Mawshwa ki thung i'a ki
mawbynna na ka bynta ban pynsah nam bad pynsah kyrteng ia ki
’riewkhraw, ki khla-ka-wait bad ’riew-donburom jong ka ri; kiwei
pat ki maw, la thung na ka bynta ban pynieng ia ka niam ka
rukom khasi; kiwei pat, na ka bynta ban pyniar i’a u pud u sam;
bad kiwei pat, na ka bynta ki jingjia kiba pher bapher. Ka jingdon
ki mawbynna ka pynphuh pynphieng i’a ka mariang, ka khring ia
ki nongjngoh kai pyrthei ban wan khmih kai bad ’ia ki riew pyrkhat
62
ban mutdur shaphang ka bor, ka jingstad bad jingshemphang Id
longshwa manshwa ban thung ia ki mawbynna.
Napdeng ki mawbynna baroh ha ri, u mawbynna uba jrong
tarn u don ha iew Nartiang lem bad kiwei kiwei ki mawbynna. U
jrong kumba phra meter shiteng, ka jingheh kumba ar meter bad
ka jingrben kumba ar meter ei ei. Ia tine ki maw la khot mawlong
syiem. La shna ia ki da u Lah Lyngskor, u Myntri u Syiem Jaifltia
bad u Mar Pharlyngki, u briew uba heh bad khlaifl.
Ha Nongkrem don uwei u mawbynna nam lem bad ar tylli
kiwei pat ki maw kiba ia syndah, uwei sha kamon bad uwei sha ka
diang. La thung ia ki ha Mawryntong harud lynti ban pyni bad
ban pynsah jingkynmaw ia ka rynieng u Shyrwad - u briew uba
jrong tam ha Nongkrem - kumba 7 Vi (Hynfliew phut shiteng).
Ha khlieh jong u la oh dur pynitynnat syriem kum ka jingspong.
Ha Laitkor don khyndai tylli kiwei pat ki jait mawbynna. Ia
uba jrong tam la pyni'eng hapdeng jong ki.
Hajan Sutnga, don ki maw shongthait kiba don laiphew tylli
ki maw shynrang bad shibun ki maw kynthei kiba iathuhkhana
pateng ba la thung ia ki ban bun nam buh kyrteng ia ka Kampatwat

kaba ioh laiphew ngut ki tnga.
Nalor kine don bun kiwei pat ki mawbynna ha ka ri Khasi
Jaifltia, kiba heh bad kiba rit, ba la thung ha ki jaka bad shnong
kyndong na ka bynta ki jingdonkam bad ki jingthmu bapher
bapher.

3. Mawkait : Uwei na ki shi hara ki mSwbynna ba ieng sha
kadiang eh na u m&wkflii.
